11. 
12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Threading.Tasks;
15. using Windows.Foundation.Collections;
16. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
17. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
18. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
19. using Windows.Storage;
20. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
21. 
22. namespace SDKTemplate
23. {
24.     public partial class MainPage : Page
25.     {
26.         public const string FEATURE_NAME = "Background Activation";
27. 
28.         List<Scenario> scenarios = new List<Scenario>
29.         {
30.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task", ClassType=typeof(SampleBackgroundTask)},
31.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Condition", ClassType=typeof(SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition)},
32.             new Scenario() { Title="Servicing Complete Task", ClassType=typeof(ServicingCompleteTask)},
33.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Time Trigger", ClassType=typeof(TimeTriggeredTask) },
34.             new Scenario() { Title="Background Task with Application Trigger", ClassType=typeof(ApplicationTriggerTask) },
35.             new Scenario() { Title="Grouped Background Task", ClassType=typeof(GroupedBackgroundTask) },
36.         };
37.     }
38. 
39.     public class Scenario
40.     {
41.         public string Title { get; set; }
42.         public Type ClassType { get; set; }
43.     }
44. }
45. 
46. namespace SDKTemplate
47. {
48.     class BackgroundTaskSample
49.     {
50.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskName = "SampleBackgroundTask";
51.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
52.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
53. 
54.         public const string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName = "SampleBackgroundTaskWithCondition";
55.         public static string SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionProgress = "";
56.         public static bool SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = false;
57. 
58.         public const string ServicingCompleteTaskName = "ServicingCompleteTask";
59.         public static string ServicingCompleteTaskProgress = "";
60.         public static bool ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = false;
61. 
62.         public const string TimeTriggeredTaskName = "TimeTriggeredTask";
63.         public static string TimeTriggeredTaskProgress = "";
64.         public static bool TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = false;
65. 
66.         public const string ApplicationTriggerTaskName = "ApplicationTriggerTask";
67.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress = "";
68.         public static string ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "";
69.         public static bool ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = false;
70. 
71.         public const string GroupedBackgroundTaskName = "GroupedBackgroundTask";
72.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupId = "3F2504E0-5F89-41D3-9A0C-0405E82C3333";
73.         public const string BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName = "Background Task Group";
74.         public static string GroupedBackgroundTaskProgress = "";
75.         public static bool GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = false;
76. 
77.         // These strings are manipulated by multiple threads, so we must
78.         // use a thread-safe container.
79.         public static PropertySet TaskStatuses = new PropertySet();
80. 
81.         /// <summary>
82.         /// Register a background task with the specified taskEntryPoint, name, trigger,
83.         /// and condition (optional).
84.         /// </summary>
85.         /// <param name="taskEntryPoint">Task entry point for the background task.</param>
86.         /// <param name="name">A name for the background task.</param>
87.         /// <param name="trigger">The trigger for the background task.</param>
88.         /// <param name="condition">An optional conditional event that must be true for the task to fire.</param>
89.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistration RegisterBackgroundTask(String taskEntryPoint, String name, IBackgroundTrigger trigger, IBackgroundCondition condition, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
90.         {
91.             if (TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(name))
92.             {
93.                 // If the user denies access, the task will not run.
94.                 var requestTask = BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
95.             }
96. 
97.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
98.             var builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
99. 
100.             builder.Name = name;
101. 
102.             if (taskEntryPoint != null)
103.             {
104.                 // If you leave the TaskEntryPoint at its default value, then the task runs
105.                 // inside the main process from OnBackgroundActivated rather than as a separate process.
106.                 builder.TaskEntryPoint = taskEntryPoint;  
107.             }
108.             
109.             builder.SetTrigger(trigger);
110. 
111.             if (condition != null)
112.             {
113.                 builder.AddCondition(condition);
114. 
115.                 //
116.                 // If the condition changes while the background task is executing then it will
117.                 // be canceled.
118.                 //
119.                 builder.CancelOnConditionLoss = true;
120.             }
121. 
122.             if (group != null)
123.             {
124.                 builder.TaskGroup = group;
125.             }
126. 
127.             BackgroundTaskRegistration task = builder.Register();
128. 
129.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, true);
130. 
131.             //
132.             // Remove previous completion status.
133.             //
134.             TaskStatuses.Remove(name);
135. 
136.             return task;
137.         }
138. 
139.         /// <summary>
140.         /// Unregister background tasks with specified name.
141.         /// </summary>
142.         /// <param name="name">Name of the background task to unregister.</param>
143.         public static void UnregisterBackgroundTasks(String name, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
144.         {
145.             //
146.             // If the given task group is registered then loop through all background tasks associated with it
147.             // and unregister any with the given name.
148.             //
149.             if (group != null)
150.             {
151.                 foreach (var cur in group.AllTasks)
152.                 {
153.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
154.                     {
155.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
156.                     }
157.                 }
158.             }
159.             else
160.             {
161.                 //
162.                 // Loop through all ungrouped background tasks and unregister any with the given name.
163.                 //
164.                 foreach (var cur in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
165.                 {
166.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
167.                     {
168.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
169.                     }
170.                 }
171.             }
172. 
173.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, false);
174.         }
175. 
176.         /// <summary>
177.         /// Retrieve a registered background task group. If no group is registered with the given id,
178.         /// then create a new one and return it.
179.         /// </summary>
180.         /// <returns>The task group associated with the given id</returns>
181.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup GetTaskGroup(string id, string groupName)
182.         {
183.             var group = BackgroundTaskRegistration.GetTaskGroup(id);
184. 
185.             if (group == null)
186.             {
187.                 group = new BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup(id, groupName);
188.             }
189. 
190.             return group;
191.         }
192. 
193.         /// <summary>
194.         /// Store the registration status of a background task with a given name.
195.         /// </summary>
196.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to store registration status for.</param>
197.         /// <param name="registered">TRUE if registered, FALSE if unregistered.</param>
198.         public static void UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(String name, bool registered)
199.         {
200.             switch (name)
201.             {
202.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
203.                     SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
204.                     break;
205.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
206.                     SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = registered;
207.                     break;
208.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
209.                     ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = registered;
210.                     break;
211.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
212.                     TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = registered;
213.                     break;
214.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
215.                     ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = registered;
216.                     break;
217.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
218.                     GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
219.                     break;
220.             }
221.         }
222. 
223.         /// <summary>
224.         /// Get the registration / completion status of the background task with
225.         /// given name.
226.         /// </summary>
227.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to retreive registration status.</param>
228.         public static String GetBackgroundTaskStatus(String name)
229.         {
230.             var registered = false;
231.             switch (name)
232.             {
233.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
234.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
235.                     break;
236.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
237.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
238.                     break;
239.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
240.                     registered = ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
241.                     break;
242.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
243.                     registered = TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
244.                     break;
245.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
246.                     registered = ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
247.                     break;
248.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
249.                     registered = GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered;
250.                     break;
251.             }
252. 
253.             var status = registered ? "Registered" : "Unregistered";
254. 
255.             object taskStatus;
256.             if (TaskStatuses.TryGetValue(name, out taskStatus))
257.             {
258.                 status += " - " + taskStatus.ToString();
259.             }
260. 
261.             return status;
262.         }
263. 
264.         /// <summary>
265.         /// Determine if task with given name requires background access.
266.         /// </summary>
267.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to query background access requirement.</param>
268.         public static bool TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(String name)
269.         {
270.             if ((name == TimeTriggeredTaskName) ||
271.                 (name == ApplicationTriggerTaskName))
272.             {
273.                 return true;
274.             }
275.             else
276.             {
277.                 return false;
278.             }
279.         }
280.     }
281. }
282. 
283. 
284. namespace SDKTemplate
285. {
286.     sealed partial class App : Application
287.     {
288.         /// <summary>
289.         /// Override the Application.OnBackgroundActivated method to handle background activation in 
290.         /// the main process. This entry point is used when BackgroundTaskBuilder.TaskEntryPoint is 
291.         /// not set during background task registration.
292.         /// </summary>
293.         /// <param name="args"></param>
294.         protected override void OnBackgroundActivated(BackgroundActivatedEventArgs args)
295.         {
296.             BackgroundActivity.Start(args.TaskInstance);
297.         }
298. 
299.         /// <summary>
300.         /// Register for grouped background task events in the Application constructor.
301.         /// </summary>
302.         partial void Construct()
303.         {
304.             var group = BackgroundTaskSample.GetTaskGroup(BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupId, BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName);
305.             group.BackgroundActivated += BackgroundActivity.Start;
306.         }
307.     }
308. }

34.         public SampleBackgroundTask()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
43.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
44.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             foreach (var task in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
47.             {
48.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName)
49.                 {
50.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
51.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName, true);
52.                     break;
53.                 }
54.             }
55. 
56.             UpdateUI();
57.         }

59.         /// <summary>

67.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName,
68.                                                                    new SystemTrigger(SystemTriggerType.TimeZoneChange, false),
69.                                                                    null);
70.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
71.             UpdateUI();
72.         }

76.         /// </summary>

78.         /// <param name="e"></param>

80.         {
81.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName);
82.             UpdateUI();
83.         }
84. 
85.         /// <summary>
86.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
87.         /// </summary>
88.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
89.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
90.         {
91.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
92.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
93.         }

97.         /// </summary>

100.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
101.         {
102.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
103.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
104.             {
105.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
106.                 BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskProgress = progress;
107.                 UpdateUI();
108.             });
109.         }

117.         {
118.             UpdateUI();
119.         }

122.         /// Update the scenario UI.
123.         /// </summary>
124.         private async void UpdateUI()
125.         {
126.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
127.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
128.             () =>
129.             {
130.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
131.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
132.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskProgress;
133.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.SampleBackgroundTaskName);
134.             });

53.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
54.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName, true);
55.                     break;
56.                 }
57.             }
58. 
59.             trigger = new ApplicationTrigger();
60.             UpdateUI();
61.         }
62. 
63.         /// <summary>
64.         /// Register a ApplicationTriggerTask.
65.         /// </summary>
66.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
67.         /// <param name="e"></param>
68.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
69.         {
70.             var task = BackgroundTaskSample.RegisterBackgroundTask(null,
71.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName,
72.                                                                    trigger,
73.                                                                    null);
74.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
75.             UpdateUI();
76.         }
77. 
78.         /// <summary>
79.         /// Unregister a ApplicationTriggerTask.
80.         /// </summary>
81.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
82.         /// <param name="e"></param>
83.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
84.         {
85.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
86.             BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "";
87.             UpdateUI();
88.         }
89. 
90.         /// <summary>
91.         /// Signal a ApplicationTriggerTask.
92.         /// </summary>
93.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
94.         /// <param name="e"></param>
95.         private async void SignalBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
96.         {
97.             // Reset the completion status
98.             BackgroundTaskSample.TaskStatuses.Remove(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
99. 
100.             //Signal the ApplicationTrigger
101.             var result = await trigger.RequestAsync();
102.             BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult = "Signal result: " + result.ToString();
103.             UpdateUI();
104.         }
105. 
106.         /// <summary>
107.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
108.         /// </summary>
109.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
110.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
111.         {
112.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
113.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
114.         }
115. 
116.         /// <summary>
117.         /// Handle background task progress.
118.         /// </summary>
119.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
120.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
121.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
122.         {
123.             // TODO[migrate-010]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
124.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
125.             {
126.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
127.                 BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress = progress;
128.                 UpdateUI();
129.             });
130.         }
131. 
132.         /// <summary>
133.         /// Handle background task completion.
134.         /// </summary>
135.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
136.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
137.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
138.         {
139.             UpdateUI();
140.         }
141. 
142.         /// <summary>
143.         /// Update the scenario UI.
144.         /// </summary>
145.         private async void UpdateUI()
146.         {
147.             // TODO[migrate-011]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
148.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
149.             () =>
150.             {
151.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
152.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
153.                 SignalButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered & (trigger != null);
154.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskProgress;
155.                 Result.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskResult;
156.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.ApplicationTriggerTaskName);
157.             });
158.         }

11. using System;
12. using System.Diagnostics;
13. using System.Threading;
14. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
15. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
16. using Windows.Foundation;
17. using Windows.Storage;
18. using Windows.System.Threading;
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     public class BackgroundActivity
23.     {
24.         BackgroundTaskCancellationReason _cancelReason = BackgroundTaskCancellationReason.Abort;
25.         volatile bool _cancelRequested = false;
26.         BackgroundTaskDeferral _deferral = null;
27.         ThreadPoolTimer _periodicTimer = null;
28.         uint _progress = 0;
29.         IBackgroundTaskInstance _taskInstance = null;
30. 
31.         //
32.         // The Run method is the entry point of a background task.
33.         //
34.         public void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
35.         {
36.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + taskInstance.Task.Name + " Starting...");
37. 
38.             //
39.             // Query BackgroundWorkCost
40.             // Guidance: If BackgroundWorkCost is high, then perform only the minimum amount
41.             // of work in the background task and return immediately.
42.             //
43.             var cost = BackgroundWorkCost.CurrentBackgroundWorkCost;
44. 
45.             //
46.             // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
47.             //
48.             taskInstance.Canceled += new BackgroundTaskCanceledEventHandler(OnCanceled);
49. 
50.             //
51.             // Get the deferral object from the task instance, and take a reference to the taskInstance;
52.             //
53.             _deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
54.             _taskInstance = taskInstance;
55. 
56.             _periodicTimer = ThreadPoolTimer.CreatePeriodicTimer(new TimerElapsedHandler(PeriodicTimerCallback), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
57.         }
58. 
59.         //
60.         // Handles background task cancellation.
61.         //
62.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
63.         {
64.             //
65.             // Indicate that the background task is canceled.
66.             //
67.             _cancelRequested = true;
68.             _cancelReason = reason;
69. 
70.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + sender.Task.Name + " Cancel Requested...");
71.         }
72. 
73.         //
74.         // Simulate the background task activity.
75.         //
76.         private void PeriodicTimerCallback(ThreadPoolTimer timer)
77.         {
78.             if ((_cancelRequested == false) && (_progress < 100))
79.             {
80.                 _progress += 10;
81.                 _taskInstance.Progress = _progress;
82.             }
83.             else
84.             {
85.                 _periodicTimer.Cancel();
86. 
87.                 var key = _taskInstance.Task.Name;
88. 
89.                 //
90.                 // Record that this background task ran.
91.                 //
92.                 String taskStatus = (_progress < 100) ? "Canceled with reason: " + _cancelReason.ToString() : "Completed";
93.                 BackgroundTaskSample.TaskStatuses[key] = taskStatus;
94.                 Debug.WriteLine("Background " + _taskInstance.Task.Name + taskStatus);
95. 
96.                 //
97.                 // Indicate that the background task has completed.
98.                 //
99.                 _deferral.Complete();
100.             }
101.         }
102. 
103.         public static void Start(BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup sender, BackgroundActivatedEventArgs args)
104.         {
105.             Start(args.TaskInstance);
106.         }
107. 
108.         public static void Start(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
109.         {
110.             // Use the taskInstance.Name and/or taskInstance.InstanceId to determine
111.             // what background activity to perform. In this sample, all of our
112.             // background activities are the same, so there is nothing to check.
113.             var activity = new BackgroundActivity();
114.             activity.Run(taskInstance);
115.         }
116.     }
117. }
118.

<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).
<exited with exit code 0>
--- RESULT ---
<a id="background-tasks"></a>
## Background Tasks`IBackgroundTask` / `BackgroundTaskRegistration` are not the recommended model. Use the WinAppSDK [`BackgroundTaskBuilder`](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/windows-app-sdk/api/winrt/microsoft.windows.applicationmodel.background.backgroundtaskbuilder) (introduced in 1.7), or move the work to push-driven activation / Windows Task Scheduler. See the [background task migration strategy](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/background-task-migration-strategy).
<exited with exit code 0>

11. 
12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using Windows.UI.Core;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;
17. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Data;
19. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;
20. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. 
22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }

11. 
12. using System;
13. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
14. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
17. 
18. // The Blank Application template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402347&clcid=0x409
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     /// <summary>
23.     /// Provides application-specific behavior to supplement the default Application class.
24.     /// </summary>
25.     sealed partial class App : Application
26.     {
27.         /// <summary>
28.         /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
29.         /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
30.         /// </summary>
31.         public App()
32.         {
33.             this.InitializeComponent();
34.             this.Construct();
35.         }
36. 
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked when the application is launched normally by the end user.  Other entry points
39.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
42.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
43.         {
44. 
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49.             }
50. #endif
51. 
52.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
53. 
54.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
55.             // just ensure that the window is active
56.             if (rootFrame == null)
57.             {
58.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
59.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
60.                 // Set the default language
61.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
62. 
63.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
64. 
65.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
66.                 {
67.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
68.                 }
69. 
70.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
71.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
72.             }
73. 
74.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
75.             {
76.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
77.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
78.                 // parameter
79.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e.Arguments);
80.             }
81. 
82.             // Ensure the current window is active
83.             Window.Current.Activate();
84. 
85.             // Some samples want access to the LaunchActivatedEventArgs.
86.             LaunchCompleted(e);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private Frame CreateRootFrame()
90.         {
91.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
92. 
93.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
94.             // just ensure that the window is active
95.             if (rootFrame == null)
96.             {
97.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
98.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
99. 
100.                 // Set the default language
101.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
102.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
103. 
104.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
105.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
106.             }
107. 
108.             return rootFrame;
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Invoked when Navigation to a certain page fails
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="sender">The Frame which failed navigation</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Details about the navigation failure</param>
116.         void OnNavigationFailed(object sender, NavigationFailedEventArgs e)
117.         {
118.             throw new Exception("Failed to load Page " + e.SourcePageType.FullName);
119.         }
120. 
121.         // Add any application constructor code in here.
122.         partial void Construct();
123. 
124.         // Add any OnLaunched customization here.
125.         partial void LaunchCompleted(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e);
126.     }
127. }

private set; }\n\n    public MainPage()\n    {\n        InitializeComponent();\n        Current = this;\n        SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;\n    }\n\n    protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)\n    {\n        var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();\n        int i = 1;\n        foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)\n        {\n            itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $\"{i++}) {s.Title}\", ClassType = s.ClassType });\n        }\n        ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;\n        ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;\n    }\n\n    private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n    {\n        NotifyUser(string.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n\n        var scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;\n        var s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;\n        if (s != null)\n        {\n            ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public List<Scenario> Scenarios => this.scenarios;\n\n    public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n    {\n        if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n        {\n            UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);\n        }\n        else\n        {\n            DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n    {\n        switch (type)\n        {\n            case NotifyType.StatusMessage:\n                StatusBorder.Background = new Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.SolidColorBrush(Microsoft.UI.Colors.Green);\n                break;\n            case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:\n                StatusBorder.Background = new Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media.SolidColorBrush(Microsoft.UI.Colors.Red);\n                break;\n        }\n\n        StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;\n        StatusBorder.Visibility = StatusBlock.Text != string.Empty ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;\n        StatusPanel.Visibility = StatusBlock.Text != string.Empty ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;\n    }\n}\n\npublic enum NotifyType\n{\n    StatusMessage,\n    ErrorMessage\n}\n```\n\n### 9. 81.         /// <summary>
82.         /// Register a background task with the specified taskEntryPoint, name, trigger,
83.         /// and condition (optional).
84.         /// </summary>
85.         /// <param name="taskEntryPoint">Task entry point for the background task.</param>
86.         /// <param name="name">A name for the background task.</param>
87.         /// <param name="trigger">The trigger for the background task.</param>
88.         /// <param name="condition">An optional conditional event that must be true for the task to fire.</param>
89.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistration RegisterBackgroundTask(String taskEntryPoint, String name, IBackgroundTrigger trigger, IBackgroundCondition condition, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
90.         {
91.             if (TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(name))
92.             {
93.                 // If the user denies access, the task will not run.
94.                 var requestTask = BackgroundExecutionManager.RequestAccessAsync();
95.             }
96. 
97.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
98.             var builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
99. 
100.             builder.Name = name;
101. 
102.             if (taskEntryPoint != null)
103.             {
104.                 // If you leave the TaskEntryPoint at its default value, then the task runs
105.                 // inside the main process from OnBackgroundActivated rather than as a separate process.
106.                 builder.TaskEntryPoint = taskEntryPoint;  
107.             }
108.             
109.             builder.SetTrigger(trigger);
110. 
111.             if (condition != null)
112.             {
113.                 builder.AddCondition(condition);
114. 
115.                 //
116.                 // If the condition changes while the background task is executing then it will
117.                 // be canceled.
118.                 //
119.                 builder.CancelOnConditionLoss = true;
120.             }
121. 
122.             if (group != null)
123.             {
124.                 builder.TaskGroup = group;
125.             }
126. 
127.             BackgroundTaskRegistration task = builder.Register();
128. 
129.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, true);
130. 
131.             //
132.             // Remove previous completion status.
133.             //
134.             TaskStatuses.Remove(name);
135. 
136.             return task;
137.         }
138. 
139.         /// <summary>
140.         /// Unregister background tasks with specified name.
141.         /// </summary>
142.         /// <param name="name">Name of the background task to unregister.</param>
143.         public static void UnregisterBackgroundTasks(String name, BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup group = null)
144.         {
145.             //
146.             // If the given task group is registered then loop through all background tasks associated with it
147.             // and unregister any with the given name.
148.             //
149.             if (group != null)
150.             {
151.                 foreach (var cur in group.AllTasks)
152.                 {
153.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
154.                     {
155.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
156.                     }
157.                 }
158.             }
159.             else
160.             {
161.                 //
162.                 // Loop through all ungrouped background tasks and unregister any with the given name.
163.                 //
164.                 foreach (var cur in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
165.                 {
166.                     if (cur.Value.Name == name)
167.                     {
168.                         cur.Value.Unregister(true);
169.                     }
170.                 }
171.             }
172. 
173.             UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(name, false);
174.         }

178.         /// then create a new one and return it.
179.         /// </summary>

181.         public static BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup GetTaskGroup(string id, string groupName)
182.         {
183.             var group = BackgroundTaskRegistration.GetTaskGroup(id);
184. 
185.             if (group == null)
186.             {
187.                 group = new BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup(id, groupName);

189. 
190.             return group;

197.         /// <param name="registered">TRUE if registered, FALSE if unregistered.</param>
198.         public static void UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(String name, bool registered)
199.         {
200.             switch (name)
201.             {
202.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
203.                     SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
204.                     break;
205.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
206.                     SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered = registered;
207.                     break;
208.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
209.                     ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered = registered;
210.                     break;
211.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
212.                     TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered = registered;
213.                     break;
214.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
215.                     ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered = registered;
216.                     break;
217.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
218.                     GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered = registered;
219.                     break;
220.             }
221.         }
222. 
223.         /// <summary>
224.         /// Get the registration / completion status of the background task with
225.         /// given name.
226.         /// </summary>
227.         /// <param name="name">Name of background task to retreive registration status.</param>
228.         public static String GetBackgroundTaskStatus(String name)
229.         {
230.             var registered = false;
231.             switch (name)
232.             {
233.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskName:
234.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskRegistered;
235.                     break;
236.                 case SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionName:
237.                     registered = SampleBackgroundTaskWithConditionRegistered;
238.                     break;
239.                 case ServicingCompleteTaskName:
240.                     registered = ServicingCompleteTaskRegistered;
241.                     break;
242.                 case TimeTriggeredTaskName:
243.                     registered = TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
244.                     break;
245.                 case ApplicationTriggerTaskName:
246.                     registered = ApplicationTriggerTaskRegistered;
247.                     break;
248.                 case GroupedBackgroundTaskName:
249.                     registered = GroupedBackgroundTaskRegistered;
250.                     break;
251.             }
252. 
253.             var status = registered ? "Registered" : "Unregistered";
254. 
255.             object taskStatus;
256.             if (TaskStatuses.TryGetValue(name, out taskStatus))
257.             {
258.                 status += " - " + taskStatus.ToString();
259.             }
260. 
261.             return status;
262.         }

264.         /// <summary>

268.         public static bool TaskRequiresBackgroundAccess(String name)
269.         {
270.             if ((name == TimeTriggeredTaskName) ||
271.                 (name == ApplicationTriggerTaskName))
272.             {
273.                 return true;
274.             }
275.             else
276.             {
277.                 return false;
278.             }
279.         }
280.     }
281. }
282. 
283. 
284. namespace SDKTemplate
285. {
286.     sealed partial class App : Application
287.     {
288.         /// <summary>
289.         /// Override the Application.OnBackgroundActivated method to handle background activation in 
290.         /// the main process. This entry point is used when BackgroundTaskBuilder.TaskEntryPoint is 
291.         /// not set during background task registration.
292.         /// </summary>
293.         /// <param name="args"></param>
294.         protected override void OnBackgroundActivated(BackgroundActivatedEventArgs args)
295.         {
296.             BackgroundActivity.Start(args.TaskInstance);
297.         }
298. 
299.         /// <summary>
300.         /// Register for grouped background task events in the Application constructor.
301.         /// </summary>
302.         partial void Construct()
303.         {
304.             var group = BackgroundTaskSample.GetTaskGroup(BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupId, BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName);
305.             group.BackgroundActivated += BackgroundActivity.Start;
306.         }
307.     }

10. 
11. using System;
12. using System.Diagnostics;
13. using System.Threading;
14. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
15. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
16. using Windows.Foundation;
17. using Windows.Storage;
18. using Windows.System.Threading;
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     public class BackgroundActivity
23.     {
24.         BackgroundTaskCancellationReason _cancelReason = BackgroundTaskCancellationReason.Abort;
25.         volatile bool _cancelRequested = false;
26.         BackgroundTaskDeferral _deferral = null;
27.         ThreadPoolTimer _periodicTimer = null;
28.         uint _progress = 0;
29.         IBackgroundTaskInstance _taskInstance = null;
30. 
31.         //
32.         // The Run method is the entry point of a background task.
33.         //
34.         public void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
35.         {
36.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + taskInstance.Task.Name + " Starting...");
37. 
38.             //
39.             // Query BackgroundWorkCost
40.             // Guidance: If BackgroundWorkCost is high, then perform only the minimum amount
41.             // of work in the background task and return immediately.
42.             //
43.             var cost = BackgroundWorkCost.CurrentBackgroundWorkCost;
44. 
45.             //
46.             // Associate a cancellation handler with the background task.
47.             //
48.             taskInstance.Canceled += new BackgroundTaskCanceledEventHandler(OnCanceled);
49. 
50.             //
51.             // Get the deferral object from the task instance, and take a reference to the taskInstance;
52.             //
53.             _deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
54.             _taskInstance = taskInstance;
55. 
56.             _periodicTimer = ThreadPoolTimer.CreatePeriodicTimer(new TimerElapsedHandler(PeriodicTimerCallback), TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1));
57.         }
58. 
59.         //
60.         // Handles background task cancellation.
61.         //
62.         private void OnCanceled(IBackgroundTaskInstance sender, BackgroundTaskCancellationReason reason)
63.         {
64.             //
65.             // Indicate that the background task is canceled.
66.             //
67.             _cancelRequested = true;
68.             _cancelReason = reason;
69. 
70.             Debug.WriteLine("Background " + sender.Task.Name + " Cancel Requested...");
71.         }
72. 
73.         //
74.         // Simulate the background task activity.
75.         //
76.         private void PeriodicTimerCallback(ThreadPoolTimer timer)
77.         {
78.             if ((_cancelRequested == false) && (_progress < 100))
79.             {
80.                 _progress += 10;
81.                 _taskInstance.Progress = _progress;
82.             }
83.             else
84.             {
85.                 _periodicTimer.Cancel();
86. 
87.                 var key = _taskInstance.Task.Name;
88. 
89.                 //
90.                 // Record that this background task ran.
91.                 //
92.                 String taskStatus = (_progress < 100) ? "Canceled with reason: " + _cancelReason.ToString() : "Completed";
93.                 BackgroundTaskSample.TaskStatuses[key] = taskStatus;
94.                 Debug.WriteLine("Background " + _taskInstance.Task.Name + taskStatus);
95. 
96.                 //
97.                 // Indicate that the background task has completed.
98.                 //
99.                 _deferral.Complete();
100.             }
101.         }
102. 
103.         public static void Start(BackgroundTaskRegistrationGroup sender, BackgroundActivatedEventArgs args)
104.         {
105.             Start(args.TaskInstance);
106.         }
107. 
108.         public static void Start(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
109.         {
110.             // Use the taskInstance.Name and/or taskInstance.InstanceId to determine
111.             // what background activity to perform. In this sample, all of our
112.             // background activities are the same, so there is nothing to check.
113.             var activity = new BackgroundActivity();
114.             activity.Run(taskInstance);
115.         }
116.     }
117. }
118.

27.     /// </summary>
28.     public sealed partial class TimeTriggeredTask : Page
29.     {
30.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
31.         // as NotifyUser()
32.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
33. 
34.         public TimeTriggeredTask()
35.         {
36.             this.InitializeComponent();
37.         }
38. 
39.         /// <summary>
40.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
43.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
44.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             foreach (var task in BackgroundTaskRegistration.AllTasks)
47.             {
48.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskName)
49.                 {
50.                     AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task.Value);
51.                     BackgroundTaskSample.UpdateBackgroundTaskRegistrationStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskName, true);
52.                     break;
53.                 }
54.             }
55. 
56.             UpdateUI();
57.         }
58. 
59.         /// <summary>
60.         /// Register a TimeTriggeredTask.
61.         /// </summary>
62.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
63.         /// <param name="e"></param>
64.         private void RegisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
65.         {
66.             var task = BackgroundTaskSample.RegisterBackgroundTask(null,
67.                                                                    BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskName,
68.                                                                    new TimeTrigger(15, false),
69.                                                                    null);
70.             AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(task);
71.             UpdateUI();
72.         }
73. 
74.         /// <summary>
75.         /// Unregister a TimeTriggeredTask.
76.         /// </summary>
77.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
78.         /// <param name="e"></param>
79.         private void UnregisterBackgroundTask(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
80.         {
81.             BackgroundTaskSample.UnregisterBackgroundTasks(BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskName);
82.             UpdateUI();
83.         }
84. 
85.         /// <summary>
86.         /// Attach progress and completed handers to a background task.
87.         /// </summary>
88.         /// <param name="task">The task to attach progress and completed handlers to.</param>
89.         private void AttachProgressAndCompletedHandlers(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task)
90.         {
91.             task.Progress += new BackgroundTaskProgressEventHandler(OnProgress);
92.             task.Completed += new BackgroundTaskCompletedEventHandler(OnCompleted);
93.         }
94. 
95.         /// <summary>
96.         /// Handle background task progress.
97.         /// </summary>
98.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting progress.</param>
99.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the progress report.</param>
100.         private void OnProgress(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskProgressEventArgs args)
101.         {
102.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
103.             var ignored = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
104.             {
105.                 var progress = "Progress: " + args.Progress + "%";
106.                 BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskProgress = progress;
107.                 UpdateUI();
108.             });
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Handle background task completion.
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="task">The task that is reporting completion.</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Arguments of the completion report.</param>
116.         private void OnCompleted(IBackgroundTaskRegistration task, BackgroundTaskCompletedEventArgs args)
117.         {
118.             UpdateUI();
119.         }
120. 
121.         /// <summary>
122.         /// Update the scenario UI.
123.         /// </summary>
124.         private async void UpdateUI()
125.         {
126.             // TODO[migrate-009]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
127.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal,
128.             () =>
129.             {
130.                 RegisterButton.IsEnabled = !BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
131.                 UnregisterButton.IsEnabled = BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskRegistered;
132.                 Progress.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskProgress;
133.                 Status.Text = BackgroundTaskSample.GetBackgroundTaskStatus(BackgroundTaskSample.TimeTriggeredTaskName);
134.             });
135.         }

37.         }

47.         {
48.             group = BackgroundTaskSample.GetTaskGroup(BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupId, BackgroundTaskSample.BackgroundTaskGroupFriendlyName);
49. 
50.             foreach (var task in group.AllTasks)
51.             {
52.                 if (task.Value.Name == BackgroundTaskSample.GroupedBackgroundTaskName)

Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;\n        StatusPanel.Visibility = StatusBlock.Text != string.Empty ?
